diff --git a/Dockerfile b/Dockerfile
index cd24432c..2ae5b574 100644
--- a/Dockerfile
+++ b/Dockerfile
@@ -3,15 +3,22 @@ FROM --platform=$BUILDPLATFORM keppel.eu-de-1.cloud.sap/hana-qa-infrastructure/i
 USER app
 WORKDIR /home/app/build
 
+ENV UV_PYTHON_DOWNLOADS=never
+
 # Cached until base image changes.
-RUN pip install --no-cache-dir uv
+RUN python -m pip install --no-cache-dir uv
 
 # Dependency layer — only rebuilds when uv.lock changes, not on source changes.
 COPY --chown=app:app pyproject.toml uv.lock ./
 # --python-platform: builder runs natively on host (may be arm64) per --platform=$BUILDPLATFORM above;
 # this overrides wheel resolution to x86_64 so the venv matches the final stage's architecture.
+# Remove any venv left on the Kaniko container filesystem by a preceding build
+# (Kaniko reuses one container for all images — leftover venvs from SP6/3.14
+# builds cause uv to query the wrong Python interpreter and fail with GLIBC errors).
 RUN rm -rf /home/app/venv && \
-    UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT=/home/app/venv uv sync --frozen --no-dev --no-install-project --python-platform x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu
+    UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT=/home/app/venv uv sync --frozen --no-dev --no-install-project \
+    --python "$(python -c 'import sys; print(sys.executable)')" \
+    --python-platform x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu
 
 # Source layer — rebuilds wheel when source changes; dep layer above stays cached.
 COPY --chown=app:app fl_shared/ ./fl_shared/

     fi
 
     if [ "$(date +%s)" -ge "$deadline" ]; then
@@ -206,13 +213,80 @@ file_container() {
   printf '%s' "$params" | jq -S -c '.data.fileContainer // {}'
 }
 
-# Report whether the service broker has observed the latest ServiceInstance spec.
-observed_generation_matches() {
+# Report whether the service broker has completed the latest ServiceInstance spec.
+# observedGeneration alone is insufficient: the SAP BTP operator can observe a
+# generation and still record Succeeded=False when Service Manager rejects or
+# times out an HDLF update.
+serviceinstance_updated() {
   expected_generation=$1
-  observed_generation=$(kubectl get serviceinstance "$SERVICE_INSTANCE" -n "$NAMESPACE" \
-    -o jsonpath='{.status.observedGeneration}' 2>/dev/null || true)
 
-  [ "$observed_generation" = "$expected_generation" ]
+  kubectl get serviceinstance "$SERVICE_INSTANCE" -n "$NAMESPACE" -o json 2>/dev/null \
+    | jq -e --argjson expected_generation "$expected_generation" '
+      (.status.conditions // [])
+      | any(
+          .type == "Succeeded"
+          and .status == "True"
+          and (.observedGeneration // 0) == $expected_generation
+        )
+    ' >/dev/null 2>&1
+}
+
+serviceinstance_update_failed() {
+  expected_generation=$1
+
+  # Once forceReconcile is set, give the BTP operator time to replace the stale
+  # failure condition with the retry result.
+  kubectl get serviceinstance "$SERVICE_INSTANCE" -n "$NAMESPACE" -o json 2>/dev/null \
+    | jq -e --argjson expected_generation "$expected_generation" '
+      ((.status.forceReconcile // false) | not)
+      and ((.status.conditions // [])
+        | any(
+            .type == "Succeeded"
+            and .status == "False"
+            and (.observedGeneration // 0) == $expected_generation
+          ))
+    ' >/dev/null 2>&1
+}
+
+serviceinstance_failure_message() {
+  expected_generation=$1
+
+  kubectl get serviceinstance "$SERVICE_INSTANCE" -n "$NAMESPACE" -o json 2>/dev/null \
+    | jq -r --argjson expected_generation "$expected_generation" '
+      .status.conditions[]?
+      | select(
+          .type == "Succeeded"
+          and .status == "False"
+          and (.observedGeneration // 0) == $expected_generation
+        )
+      | "\(.reason): \(.message)"
+    ' 2>/dev/null \
+    | head -n 1 || true
+}
+
+broker_reconcile_complete() {
+  expected_generation=$1
+
+  if serviceinstance_updated "$expected_generation"; then
+    return 0
+  fi
+
+  if serviceinstance_update_failed "$expected_generation"; then
+    echo "ServiceInstance ${SERVICE_INSTANCE} update failed: $(serviceinstance_failure_message "$expected_generation")" >&2
+    return 2
+  fi
+
+  return 1
+}
+
+force_serviceinstance_reconcile() {
+  echo "Requesting BTP operator retry for ServiceInstance ${SERVICE_INSTANCE}..."
+  # status.forceReconcile is the SAP BTP operator's retry switch for failed
+  # ServiceInstance operations. Patching status avoids mutating desired state.
+  kubectl patch serviceinstance "$SERVICE_INSTANCE" -n "$NAMESPACE" \
+    --subresource=status \
+    --type=merge \
+    -p '{"status":{"forceReconcile":true}}'
 }
 
 # Wait for the broker to apply the patched fileContainer configuration.
@@ -223,7 +297,7 @@ wait_for_broker_reconcile() {
   wait_until "broker reconciliation for ServiceInstance generation ${generation}" \
     "$RECONCILE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS" \
     "$RETRY_INTERVAL_SECONDS" \
-    observed_generation_matches "$generation"
+    broker_reconcile_complete "$generation"
 }
 
 # Patch HDLF trust configuration only when the desired fileContainer state differs.
@@ -232,8 +306,16 @@ reconcile_file_container() {
   before_params=$(current_params)
   after_params=$(desired_params "$before_params" "$ca_crt")
 
+  generation=$(kubectl get serviceinstance "$SERVICE_INSTANCE" -n "$NAMESPACE" \
+    -o jsonpath='{.metadata.generation}')
+
   if [ "$(file_container "$before_params")" = "$(file_container "$after_params")" ]; then
     echo "HDLF fileContainer trust, roles, and authorizations already match."
+    # If the desired spec already matches, a normal spec patch would be a no-op
+    # and would not make the BTP operator retry a stale failed HDLF update.
+    if serviceinstance_update_failed "$generation"; then
+      force_serviceinstance_reconcile
+    fi
   else
     echo "Patching HDLF fileContainer trust, roles, and authorizations..."
     patch=$(jq -c -n --argjson params "$after_params" '{spec: {parameters: $params}}')

diff --git a/fl_mcp_servers/Dockerfile b/fl_mcp_servers/Dockerfile
index 412868b7..65b79ffe 100644
--- a/fl_mcp_servers/Dockerfile
+++ b/fl_mcp_servers/Dockerfile
@@ -3,15 +3,22 @@ FROM --platform=$BUILDPLATFORM keppel.eu-de-1.cloud.sap/hana-qa-infrastructure/i
 USER app
 WORKDIR /home/app/build
 
+ENV UV_PYTHON_DOWNLOADS=never
+
 # Cached until base image changes.
-RUN pip install --no-cache-dir uv
+RUN python -m pip install --no-cache-dir uv
 
 # Dependency layer — only rebuilds when uv.lock changes, not on source changes.
 COPY --chown=app:app fl_mcp_servers/pyproject.toml fl_mcp_servers/uv.lock ./
 # --python-platform: builder runs natively on host (may be arm64) per --platform=$BUILDPLATFORM above;
 # this overrides wheel resolution to x86_64 so the venv matches the final stage's architecture.
+# Remove any venv left on the Kaniko container filesystem by a preceding build
+# (Kaniko reuses one container for all images — leftover venvs from SP6/3.14
+# builds cause uv to query the wrong Python interpreter and fail with GLIBC errors).
 RUN rm -rf /home/app/venv && \
-    UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT=/home/app/venv uv sync --frozen --no-dev --no-install-project --python-platform x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu
+    UV_PROJECT_ENVIRONMENT=/home/app/venv uv sync --frozen --no-dev --no-install-project \
+    --python "$(python -c 'import sys; print(sys.executable)')" \
+    --python-platform x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu
 
 # Source layer — rebuilds wheel when source changes; dep layer above stays cached.
 COPY --chown=app:app fl_control_plane/ ./fl_control_plane/
@@ -33,5 +40,5 @@ ENV PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1

 # which server to start via the command, e.g.:
-#   python -m fl_mcp_servers.pipeline_data_mcp
-#   python -m fl_mcp_servers.inspection_results_mcp
+#   python -m fl_mcp_servers.generic.pipeline_data_mcp
+#   python -m fl_mcp_servers.generic.inspection_results_mcp

diff --git a/tests/execution_engine/test_hdlf_cert_provisioner.py b/tests/execution_engine/test_hdlf_cert_provisioner.py
index 63a9ff0f..2d9230ad 100644
--- a/tests/execution_engine/test_hdlf_cert_provisioner.py
+++ b/tests/execution_engine/test_hdlf_cert_provisioner.py
@@ -2,6 +2,8 @@
 
 from __future__ import annotations
 
+from collections.abc import Iterator
+from contextlib import contextmanager
 from types import SimpleNamespace
 from unittest.mock import AsyncMock
 
@@ -12,9 +14,95 @@
 from fl_control_plane.execution_engine import hdlf_cert_provisioner
 
 
+class _FakeCustomObjectsApi:
+    def __init__(self, certificates: list[dict[str, object]]) -> None:
+        self.get_namespaced_custom_object = AsyncMock(side_effect=certificates)
+
+
+def _record_log_contexts(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
+    """Record structlog context bindings made by the code under test."""
+    contexts: list[dict[str, object]] = []
+
+    @contextmanager
+    def _bound_contextvars(**kwargs: object) -> Iterator[None]:
+        contexts.append(kwargs)
+        yield
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(
+        hdlf_cert_provisioner.structlog.contextvars,
+        "bound_contextvars",
+        _bound_contextvars,
+    )
+    return contexts
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_poll_certificate_waits_while_secret_does_not_exist(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
+    """Secret absence during issuance is treated as in-progress, not failed."""
+    contexts = _record_log_contexts(monkeypatch)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(hdlf_cert_provisioner, "_CERT_POLL_INTERVAL_SECONDS", 0)
+    custom = _FakeCustomObjectsApi(
+        [
+            {
+                "status": {
+                    "conditions": [
+                        {
+                            "type": "Ready",
+                            "status": "False",
+                            "reason": "DoesNotExist",
+                            "message": "Issuing certificate as Secret does not exist",
+                        }
+                    ]
+                }
+            },
+            {"status": {"conditions": [{"type": "Ready", "status": "True"}]}},
+        ]
+    )
+
+    await hdlf_cert_provisioner._poll_certificate(  # type: ignore[arg-type]
+        custom,
+        "fl-job-exec-1-hdlf-cert",
+        "test-depl-name",
+        "insp-1",
+    )
+
+    assert custom.get_namespaced_custom_object.await_count == 2
+    assert contexts == [{"inspection_id": "insp-1"}]
+
+
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_poll_certificate_fails_on_terminal_ready_false_reason() -> None:
+    """Explicit terminal certificate Ready=False reasons fail fast."""
+    custom = _FakeCustomObjectsApi(
+        [
+            {
+                "status": {
+                    "conditions": [
+                        {
+                            "type": "Ready",
+                            "status": "False",
+                            "reason": "Failed",
+                            "message": "private key generation failed",
+                        }
+                    ]
+                }
+            }
+        ]
+    )
+
+    with pytest.raises(IOError, match="failed: Failed"):
+        await hdlf_cert_provisioner._poll_certificate(  # type: ignore[arg-type]
+            custom,
+            "fl-job-exec-1-hdlf-cert",
+            "test-depl-name",
+            "insp-1",
+        )
+
+
 @pytest.mark.asyncio
 async def test_provision_inspection_access_grants_read_write_policy(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
     """Per-inspection handler certs can read inputs and write handler results."""
+    contexts = _record_log_contexts(monkeypatch)
 
     class _FakeHdlfClient:
         instances: list["_FakeHdlfClient"] = []
@@ -54,6 +142,7 @@ async def __aexit__(self, *_args: object) -> None:
     secret_name = await hdlf_cert_provisioner.provision_inspection_access("insp-1", "exec-1")
 
     assert secret_name == "fl-job-exec-1-hdlf-cert"
+    assert contexts[0] == {"inspection_id": "insp-1"}
     _FakeHdlfClient.instances[0].create_policy.assert_awaited_once_with(
         policy_name="fl-inspection-insp-1",
         path="/insp-1/",
@@ -75,6 +164,38 @@ async def __aexit__(self, *_args: object) -> None:
     assert _FakeHdlfClient.instances[1].kwargs["client_key"].get_secret_value() == "key"
 
 
+@pytest.mark.asyncio
+async def test_patch_certificate_owner_binds_inspection_log_context(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
+    """Certificate owner patching binds inspection_id for structured log filtering."""
+    contexts = _record_log_contexts(monkeypatch)
+    patch_namespaced_custom_object = AsyncMock()
+
+    class _FakeApiClient:
+        async def __aenter__(self) -> object:
+            return object()
+
+        async def __aexit__(self, *_args: object) -> None:
+            return None
+
+    class _FakeCustomObjectsApi:
+        def __init__(self, _api_client: object) -> None:
+            self.patch_namespaced_custom_object = patch_namespaced_custom_object
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(hdlf_cert_provisioner.client, "ApiClient", _FakeApiClient)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(hdlf_cert_provisioner.client, "CustomObjectsApi", _FakeCustomObjectsApi)
+
+    await hdlf_cert_provisioner.patch_certificate_owner(
+        execution_id="exec-1",
+        inspection_id="insp-1",
+        namespace="test-depl-name",
+        job_uid="job-uid-1",
+    )
+
+    assert contexts == [{"inspection_id": "insp-1"}]
+    patch_namespaced_custom_object.assert_awaited_once()
+    assert patch_namespaced_custom_object.await_args.kwargs["name"] == "fl-job-exec-1-hdlf-cert"
+
+
 def _assert_access_probe(fake_client: object, inspection_id: str) -> None:
     """Assert the HDLF readiness probe writes, stats, reads, and deletes a temp file."""
     put_call = fake_client.put_object.await_args  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
